# Albedo study: trend attribution summary

Trends per decade with 2σ (lag-1 adjusted; for A_types_air_dt, A_types_scav_dt, A_mass_dt combined in quadrature with a 100-member year-block bootstrap of the fitted sensitivities). A = ridge, sulphate only; A_types = ridge, all aerosol types; B = LightGBM, all types (shap_ = SHAP share, cf_ = counterfactual with that aerosol type held at 2003-2005 levels). Skill = out-of-sample R² of the region-mean series.
